=== docs/RUBRIC.md ===
# System Portfolio & Walkthrough Standardization Rubric (v2.3)

This document establishes the official design, presentation, UI, terminology, architectural justification, and governance standards for all portfolio web assets (`public/index.html`), demonstration applications (`server.js`), and generated media walkthroughs (`scripts/generate-walkthrough-gif.js`).

---

## 1. Core Principles & Continual Learning Protocol

### **1.1 Continual Learning & Versioning**
- Every future session that modifies web assets, walkthrough scripts, or demonstration pages **MUST** read `docs/RUBRIC.md` and `LESSONS_LEARNED.md` to verify full compliance.
- When new capabilities, layout improvements, or solution patterns are merged into the repository, the active session **MUST** update `docs/RUBRIC.md` and increment the version (e.g., `v2.1` → `v2.2`).
- Historical PRs, git logs, and `docs/plans/` serve as training data for future agents to continuously improve solution quality.

### **1.2 Mandatory Wireframes Before Implementation**
- Before starting any software or script implementation that affects visual UI, presentation, or media outputs, the agent **MUST** present drawn text wireframes of the BEFORE and AFTER states to the user during planning and obtain explicit confirmation.

### **1.3 Solution Choice Justification Rule (Process of Elimination & Best Practice)**
- Every job role demo **MUST** explicitly explain and justify why its specific architecture choices were selected over all existing real-world AI and automation alternatives. The walkthrough MUST detail how micro-solution components fit together to resolve small operational friction points and solve the macro profit leak.
- **Modern AI & Automation Solutions Evaluated by Process of Elimination**:
  1. **Naive Dense RAG (Vector Search Only)**:
     - *Elimination Reason*: Fails on exact keyword and ID matching (e.g., confuses part numbers or requisition IDs like `#PR-496-082` with `#PR-496-083`).
  2. **Fine-Tuned LLM (Custom Model Weights)**:
     - *Elimination Reason*: Extremely expensive to train/maintain, slow to update when supplier master contracts change, and carries high hallucination risks on exact financial numbers.
  3. **Knowledge Graph / GraphRAG / Ontologies**:
     - *Elimination Reason*: High setup overhead for unstructured single-document quote parsing; best reserved for multi-entity relationship mapping rather than fast document extraction.
  4. **Agentic Swarms (Autonomous Multi-Agent Systems)**:
     - *Elimination Reason*: Introduces non-deterministic execution loops, latency, and unpredictable financial state shifts for deterministic transactional workflows.
  5. **Pure Zero-Shot / Few-Shot LLM Prompting**:
     - *Elimination Reason*: Limited context window for multi-page contract evaluation and lacks grounding against master contract tables.
  6. **Hybrid RAG (Dense Vector Search + Sparse Lexical Keyword Search - Qdrant + BM25)**:
     - *Selected Best Practice for Unstructured Quote Parsing*: Combines dense vector embeddings (to understand legal clause risk and T&C semantics) with sparse lexical search (to match exact part numbers and requisition IDs with 100% precision).
  7. **Deterministic Rules Engine (SAP Ariba P2P Engine)**:
     - *Selected Best Practice for Order Execution & Accounting*: Guarantees 100% zero-variance arithmetic and auditability for financial commitments (Purchase Order == Goods Receipt Note == Vendor Invoice). AI is strictly prevented from executing order placement directly.

### **1.4 Mandatory Process-of-Elimination End-to-End Walkthrough Step**
- The walkthrough MUST present a step-by-step end-to-end example explaining how each optimal solution component resolves a specific micro-problem:
  - *Micro-Problem 1 (Unstructured Text & Risk)*: AI Hybrid RAG parses 14-page supplier PDF quote, extracting unit prices and flagging hidden fine-print 14-week lead-time delay clauses on Page 12.
  - *Micro-Problem 2 (Financial Reconciliations)*: Deterministic Rules Engine enforces LTA master contract rates ($92/unit), checks buyer spending limit SLA (<$100,000), and executes SAP 3-Way Invoice Match.
  - *Macro-Problem Solved (Profit Leak Elimination)*: Cuts purchasing turn times from 72 hours down to 1.5 hours, preventing factory assembly downtime and saving $9.3 Million in annual indirect spend.

### **1.5 Definition of "Completing the Project" Standard**
- "Completing the project" requires executing the entire end-to-end software factory pipeline:
  1. **Job Text Registration**: Updating `job.txt` with target requisition text.
  2. **Rubric Governance**: Updating `docs/RUBRIC.md` with session learning and rules.
  3. **Queue Synthesis**: Creating `_job_queue/<REQ_ID>.json` with job-tailored financial & operational metrics.
  4. **Workspace Demo Application**: Updating `server.js` with job-specific workflows and UI mockups.
  5. **Headless Media Recording**: Running `scripts/generate-walkthrough-gif.js` to record `<REQ_ID>.gif`.
  6. **Portfolio & Resume Compilation**: Running `scripts/compile-portfolio.js` to update `public/index.html` and compile the tailored markdown resume in `dist_resumes/`.
  7. **Frontend Verification**: Performing Playwright screenshot verification and registering media completion via `frontend_verification_complete`.

---

## 2. Terminology, Layout & Visual Standardization Rules

### **2.1 Plain English Rule & Prohibition of Explanatory Meta-Phrases**
- All UI text, terminal stream logs, status banners, and card titles **MUST** be written in simple, clear English so recruiters, hiring managers, and civilians without deep technical backgrounds understand the value immediately.
- Meta-phrases such as `"Explained in Simple English"` or `"Civilian Accessible"` **MUST NEVER** appear as text in the UI or walkthroughs. Content must simply be written cleanly without self-referential meta-labels.

### **2.2 Domain Acronym Definitions & Standalone Section 5 Appendix**
- Whenever industry or domain-specific acronyms, software terms, or technical phrases are used (e.g., `WCAG`, `AEM EDS`, `Modo Labs`, `OneCampus`, `ARIA Landmarks`, `Qdrant`, `BM25`, `P2P`, `PO`, `PR`, `SLA`), they **MUST** be defined in a dedicated, standalone **Section 5 Appendix** view at the end of the walkthrough.
- **Standalone Appendix Rule**: The Appendix view MUST render on its own clean screen display (Section 5 of 5) without overlapping or cluttering application content from previous sections.
- **Civilian Accessibility & Real-World Example Rule**: All definitions in the Section 5 Appendix **MUST** be written in simple, plain English without technical jargon, using concrete real-world civilian examples (e.g., explaining WCAG as "wheelchair ramps for websites" or ARIA landmarks as "Braille signs for screen readers") so that non-technical managers and civilians can understand all terms instantly.

### **2.3 Outer Standardized Main Window vs. Inner Software UI Mockup Window**
- **Outer Main Window**: The outer main window frame MUST be 100% generic, standardized, and consistent across ALL job cards. It contains only standard title headers, top-left section labels (`SECTION X OF Y`), and high-level section explanations. It MUST NOT contain job-specific text (such as company names or requisition IDs in the outer frame).
- **Inner Software UI Mockup Window**: The software UI mockup (e.g., SAP Ariba UI clone for Procurement, or Lockheed UI clone for M&A) MUST be rendered INSIDE an inner window frame within the main window. ALL job-specific details (company name, plant site, requisition IDs, supplier names) belong strictly inside the inner software mockup window.

### **2.4 Standardized Top-Left Section Step Labels**
- Every demo slide/screen across ALL job cards MUST feature the `SECTION X OF Y` label positioned consistently in the **TOP-LEFT CORNER** of the outer main window (e.g., `SECTION 1 OF 5`, `SECTION 2 OF 5`, `SECTION 3 OF 5`, `SECTION 4 OF 5`, `SECTION 5 OF 5`), completely detached and independent from any inner content box or modal card.
- The `SECTION X OF Y` label MUST maintain identical font size, font family (`font-mono`), color (`text-cyan-400`), weight (`font-bold`), and uppercase tracking across all sections and title cards.

### **2.5 Permanent Removal of Terminal Streams & Clean Expanded Panels**
- Terminal streams and raw terminal log boxes are **PERMANENTLY REMOVED** from all demo template code and workspace applications (`server.js`). Terminals do not convey useful information for executive viewers or hiring managers.
- In place of terminal streams, execution result containers **MUST** be expanded (`min-h-[160px]` / `max-h-[220px]`, with internal `overflow-y-auto`) to display clear, high-contrast operational summaries, key performance indicators, and structured outcomes without empty whitespace.

### **2.13 KPI Chips Placement Rule**
- Standardized 6-KPI Chips Grid **MUST ONLY** appear inside the Section 1 Executive Brief section view (`mode=section1`) and **MUST NOT** be rendered on Section 1 Title Cards or duplicated across any other title cards or demo slides.

### **2.14 Architectural Justification Placement Rule**
- The Process of Elimination Architectural Justification text **MUST ONLY** display on the Section 4 Title Slide in the walkthrough script (`scripts/generate-walkthrough-gif.js`) and **MUST NOT** be rendered inside the Section 4 Demo Slide layout in `server.js`.

### **2.15 Inner Mockup Space Preservation Rule**
- Inner software UI mockup windows **MUST NOT** include horizontal tab navigation headers or redundant sub-navigation bars. Removing tab rows preserves vertical screen real estate and guarantees zero text clipping inside the 1280x720 video frame.

### **2.16 Single Consolidated Workflow Panel & Labeled Step Bar Rule**
- Inner software UI mockups **MUST** feature a **Single Consolidated Panel** containing a visual, labeled workflow progress bar (e.g. `Step 1: REFramework Check` → `Step 2: Document AI Extraction` → `Step 3: Action Center Auto-Approval`) driven by a single unified execution button.
- Separate legacy error simulation slides/demo sections are **PERMANENTLY ELIMINATED**. All walkthroughs jump directly to the Consolidated Optimized Solution view.

### **2.17 Micro & Macro Solution Results Container Placement Rule**
- The expanded execution results container inside the single consolidated panel **MUST** display the complete operational execution output, including:
  1. All **Micro-Solution** details mapped directly to their corresponding legacy micro-problems using plain English scenarios (without technical jargon or analogies).
  2. The **Macro Solution Summary** showing how all micro-solutions combine into a unified workflow to achieve target SLAs and cost savings.

### **2.18 Micro-Solution Formatting & Legacy Pain Terminology Rule**
- Micro-solutions in the results container **MUST** be formatted and labeled strictly as `Micro-Solution X: [Title]` (e.g., `Micro-Solution 1: REFramework Automated Error Recovery & Health Checks`).
- The phrasing `Micro-Problem X vs Micro-Solution X` or leading with `Micro-Problem X` is **STRICTLY PROHIBITED**.
- Phrasing for legacy system failures must strictly use `Legacy Pain:` and **MUST NOT** include the phrase `"Without this,"`.

### **2.6 Universal Dynamic Text Scaling & Zero Vertical Cutoff Invariant**
- **Strict Zero-Clipping Rule**: All UI elements, cards, chips, containers, and text font sizes **MUST** be dynamically styled and sized to guarantee zero vertical clipping or bottom text cutoffs within the standard 1280x720 video walkthrough viewport frame.
- **Dynamic Font & Content Scaling**: All text font sizes (`text-[10px]` through `text-sm`) and chip containers **MUST** adjust and scale dynamically depending on surrounding content. If a slide or section contains additional descriptive text, chip sizes, text sizes, and padding (`p-2` to `p-2.5`) MUST shrink dynamically so that all content fits cleanly inside the viewport without pushing elements off-screen.

### **2.6 Zero Commingling of Previous Demo Content & Terminology**
- Content or terminology from previous job role demos (e.g., "Target Entity", "M&A", "Deal Target", "Deal Brief", or "Subordinated Notes" from Lockheed) **MUST NEVER** appear in new job role demos (such as Procurement or Engineering). Each demo must be 100% cleanly scoped to its specific target job role domain.
- **Strict Single-Job Focus Rule**: The walkthrough demo, terminal streams, and Section 5 Appendix for a job card MUST ONLY contain content and data directly related to that specific job. Referencing previous job cards or comparisons to other portfolio cards in the demo output is strictly prohibited.

### **2.11 Mandatory Executive Brief Sub-Components**
- **Executive Brief Requirements**: The Executive Brief section (`SECTION 1 OF 5 | EXECUTIVE BRIEF`) **MUST** always contain three mandatory sub-components:
  1. *Risk Analysis Matrix*: Highlighting Specific Risk Identified, Why It Matters, and How To Eliminate Risk.
  2. *Benchmark Comparison Chart*: Visualizing legacy vs optimized SLA/turn times.
  3. *Executive Recommendation*: A clear, actionable executive conclusion.

### **2.12 Technical Stack Deep Dive Appendix Section**
- **Detailed Solution Architecture Section**: Following the Appendix, an additional technical details section **MUST** be included detailing the inner software workings and technical architecture of the optimized solution stack in plain, clear English (without relying on analogies), so non-technical stakeholders can understand exactly how the software components operate under the hood.

### **2.7 Standardized Reusable UI Titles vs. Job-Specific Demo Mockups**
- Webpage job cards on Git pages MUST feature standardized, job-independent outer card containers and standard section titles (`SECTION 1 OF 5 | AUDIT BRIEF`, `SECTION 2 OF 5 | LEGACY WORKFLOW`, `SECTION 3 OF 5 | OPTIMIZED WORKFLOW`, `SECTION 4 OF 5 | EXECUTIVE BRIEF`, `SECTION 5 OF 5 | APPENDIX & KEY TERMS GUIDE`).
- While outer card layouts and main section titles are standardized across all cards, the inner UI titles, software UI mockups, and specific operational data inside the inner windows MUST be built from scratch for every unique job role demo card.

### **2.7 Surface Mirroring & Repeat Consistency Invariant**
- **Cross-Surface Mirroring**: Exact identical wording, labels, and phrases must be used across BOTH the Git Webpage (`public/index.html`) and the Walkthrough Video (`.gif`).
- **Within-Surface Repeat Consistency**: Every repeated mention of a metric, label, or concept on the webpage or in the walkthrough video must use the **exact same phrasing** as its first occurrence.

### **2.8 Standardized Baseline Metrics Vocabulary**
The following canonical metric labels **MUST** be used verbatim whenever these values appear:
- `LEGACY OPERATING COST` (Pre-optimization manual workflow cost)
- `OPTIMIZED OPERATING COST` (Post-optimization automated workflow cost)
- `NET ANNUAL SAVINGS` (Calculated as `LEGACY OPERATING COST - OPTIMIZED OPERATING COST`)
- `SOLUTION INVESTMENT` (Calculated baseline cost to build, verify, and deploy the solution)
- `RETURN ON INVESTMENT` (Calculated as `(NET ANNUAL SAVINGS / SOLUTION INVESTMENT) * 100`)
- `EVM DELIVERABILITY` (Project control metrics: Cost Performance Index `CPI` and Schedule Performance Index `SPI`)
- `SECTION 1 OF 5 | AUDIT BRIEF`
- `SECTION 2 OF 5 | LEGACY WORKFLOW`
- `SECTION 3 OF 5 | OPTIMIZED WORKFLOW`
- `SECTION 4 OF 5 | EXECUTIVE BRIEF`
- `SECTION 5 OF 5 | APPENDIX & KEY TERMS GUIDE`

---

## 3. Walkthrough Video Presentation Standards (`scripts/generate-walkthrough-gif.js`)

### **3.1 Mandatory 5-Section Flow & URL Mode Control**
Every generated walkthrough video **MUST** follow a standardized 5-section structure, navigated via direct URL mode parameters (`?mode=section1`, `?mode=section2`, `?mode=section3`, `?mode=section4`, `?mode=appendix`):
1. **Section 1: System Target Metrics Brief** (`?mode=section1`) (Title Card + Standardized 6-KPI Chips Grid)
2. **Section 2: Legacy Workflow Baseline Simulation** (`?mode=section2`) (Title Card + Legacy Workflow Panel with `SECTION 2 OF 5 | LEGACY WORKFLOW` Top-Left Cyan Label & Process Failure Crash)
3. **Section 3: Optimized Workflow Simulation** (`?mode=section3`) (Title Card + Side-by-Side AI & Rule Engine Panels with `SECTION 3 OF 5 | OPTIMIZED WORKFLOW` Top-Left Cyan Label)
4. **Section 4: Executive Brief & Benchmark Analysis** (`?mode=section4`) (Title Card + Dedicated Viewport Centered Executive Brief & Benchmark Chart with `SECTION 4 OF 5 | EXECUTIVE BRIEF` Top-Left Cyan Label)
5. **Section 5: Key Terms Appendix** (`?mode=appendix`) (Title Card + Standalone Glossary & Key Terms Guide with `SECTION 5 OF 5 | APPENDIX & KEY TERMS GUIDE` Top-Left Cyan Label)

### **3.2 Complete Segregation of Title Cards**
- Every section's Title Card **MUST** be rendered on its own standalone, clean view (centered on screen, zero clutter, 100% segregated without any section application content showing).
- Title cards must display top step indicators: `SECTION 1 OF 5`, `SECTION 2 OF 5`, `SECTION 3 OF 5`, `SECTION 4 OF 5`, `SECTION 5 OF 5`.

### **3.3 Dedicated Viewport Centering Across ALL Sections**
- ALL application content frames across Section 1, Section 2, Section 3, Section 4, and Section 5 **MUST** be rendered with dedicated full-viewport centering (1280x720 viewport), zero page scrollbars, and zero upper/lower screen clutter.

### **3.4 Section 1 Standardized 6-KPI Chips Grid**
- Section 1 **MUST** use a uniform grid of 6 distinct KPI chips with high-contrast borders and clear subtitles:
  1. `LEGACY OPERATING COST`
  2. `OPTIMIZED OPERATING COST`
  3. `NET ANNUAL SAVINGS`
  4. `SOLUTION INVESTMENT`
  5. `RETURN ON INVESTMENT`
  6. `EVM DELIVERABILITY`

### **3.5 Workspace Focus & Requisition Exclusion**
- Requisition IDs (`REQ ID`) and Job Titles belong on the Git Webpage job cards directory and **MUST NOT** appear in the video walkthrough frames.

### **3.6 UI Clones / Mockups for Job SaaS Tools**
- If a target job description specifies tools or SaaS platforms (e.g., SAP, SAP Ariba, Oracle, Salesforce), the demonstration workspace application **MUST** render styled UI mockups/clones of those interfaces inside an inner window frame.

---

## 4. Git Webpage & Job Card Directory Standards (`public/index.html` & `scripts/compile-portfolio.js`)

### **4.1 Single-Line Job Card Header**
- Each job card in the directory **MUST** feature a compact single-line header strip containing:
  `Company Name • Job Title ... REQ ID: XXXXX`

### **4.2 Real-time Instant Filter & Smooth Jump**
- Search input filters non-matching cards instantly as the user types.
- Pressing `Enter` or clicking the `Jump ↵` button smoothly scrolls the viewport to the top matching card.

### **4.3 Clean Empty State Container**
- When search query returns 0 matching cards (e.g., `xyz999`), the page **MUST NOT** remain blank. It must render a styled dashed container:
  `🔍 No Business Solutions Found. No matching walkthrough entries found for 'xyz999'. Try searching company name, job title, or REQ ID.`

### **4.4 Minimalist & Responsive Design**
- High-contrast slate/cyan/emerald Tailwind design.
- Fully responsive across desktop, tablet, and mobile layouts.

About the job
Role and Responsibilities:

Your specific duties will be based on your experience as Technical Account Manager, and you will be an extended part of the client’s technical team, ensuring automation solutions are running effectively. In this role, you will be responsible for designing and delivering UiPath solutions in accordance with WonderBotz standards and best practices. You will work closely with our enthusiastic business and technical specialists team. You will be part of a fast-growing and successful team that helps our clients get the maximum benefit.

Expected Activities:

Support for customers to support pre-sales and post-sales processes
Provide developers with customers’ feedback and contribute to the identification and creation of new features or products
Perform initial or secondary investigations and respond to online and phone support requests
Deliver periodic reviews with customers (e.g., Health checks) and proactively analyze customers’ needs and suggest upgrades or additional features to meet their requirements
Collaborate cross-departmentally within UiPath to establish and communicate best practices
Build and manage a passionate, customer-centric, outcome-driven team to deliver upon your portfolio
Ensure the quality of your team's service delivery, operations, and relationship management
Provide mentoring, career development, and performance management to guide the continuous improvement of your team

Qualifications And Skills:

Have mastered or strongly desired to master a leading RPA tool (UiPath a must, Blue Prism, Automation Anywhere), including advanced RPA vendor certification
At least one year of hands-on experience with at least one of the following programming languages (e.g., .Net, Java, VB, C#/C, HTML/CSS, Python, Web Services, mainframe, web applications, SQL, data integration tools, technical automation tools). More senior developers should have a minimum of 2 to 4 years of this hands-on experience
Reasonably proficiency in reading Microsoft Office Visio or other equivalent process flow-charting tools or workflow-based logic
Extra - Any prior work or academic experience with Document management and processing tools (e.g., Kofax, ABBYY, Data Cap), Data integration tools (e.g., Informatica, Microsoft SSIS), Technical automation tools (e.g., shell scripting, PHP), or Business process management tools (e.g., Pega)

Desired characteristics in candidates:

Demonstrate excellent written and verbal communication skills
Passion to work and learn fast in a fast-paced, high-energy, and challenging environment with peers who challenge you to improve
Thrive to learn and improve sales and marketing skills
Has proven analytical and problem-solving skills
Is highly self-driven with a sense of motivation
Has been both a team player and an individual contributor

Compensation and start dates:

Hiring now for immediate start
Salary: Competitive base and bonus determined by level and experience
Benefits: Healthcare, relocation, vacation, holidays

WonderBotz is an Equal Employment Opportunity employer.

=== scripts/compile-portfolio.js ===
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

// UTF-8 safe base64 helpers for GitHub API (handles Unicode like · in history_registry.json)
function utf8ToBase64(str) {
    const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(str);
    let binary = '';
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) binary += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);
    return btoa(binary);
}

function base64ToUtf8(b64) {
    const binary = atob(b64.replace(/\s/g, ''));
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
    return new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);
}

const queueDir = path.join(__dirname, '../_job_queue');
const publicDir = path.join(__dirname, '../public');
const historyFilePath = path.join(__dirname, '../_job_queue/history_registry.json');
const templatePath = path.join(__dirname, '../resume_template.md');
const resumeOutputDir = path.join(__dirname, '../dist_resumes');

if (!fs.existsSync(publicDir)) fs.mkdirSync(publicDir, { recursive: true });
if (!fs.existsSync(queueDir)) fs.mkdirSync(queueDir, { recursive: true });
if (!fs.existsSync(resumeOutputDir)) fs.mkdirSync(resumeOutputDir, { recursive: true });

let historicalCases = [];
if (fs.existsSync(historyFilePath)) {
  try {
    historicalCases = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(historyFilePath, 'utf8'));
  } catch (err) {
    historicalCases = [];
  }
}

// Ingest newly generated profiles dropped by Jules
const jobFiles = fs.readdirSync(queueDir).filter(f => f.endsWith('.json') && f !== 'history_registry.json' && f !== 'schema.json');

jobFiles.forEach(file => {
  const filePath = path.join(queueDir, file);
  try {
    const fileData = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8'));
    // Default gifAsset/mediaAsset to docs/assets/{reqId}.gif if not specified and reqId exists
    if (!fileData.mediaAsset && !fileData.gifAsset && fileData.reqId) {
      fileData.gifAsset = `docs/assets/${fileData.reqId.toLowerCase()}.gif`;
    }
    
    if (!historicalCases.some(item => item.reqId === fileData.reqId)) {
      historicalCases.unshift(fileData);
    }
    fs.unlinkSync(filePath);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`[Error] Ingestion failure on ${file}:`, err.message);
  }
});

fs.writeFileSync(historyFilePath, JSON.stringify(historicalCases, null, 2));

// Generate the customized resume targets with macro interpolation 
if (fs.existsSync(templatePath)) {
  const templateContent = fs.readFileSync(templatePath, 'utf8');
  historicalCases.forEach(job => {
    const safeCompName = job.companyName.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '_');
    const resumeFileName = `Resume_${safeCompName}_${job.reqId || 'standalone'}.md`;
    const tailoredResume = templateContent.replace(/{{JOB_REQ_ID}}/g, job.reqId || '');
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(resumeOutputDir, resumeFileName), tailoredResume);
  });
}

// SVG Icons

const emailIcon = `<svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 4h16c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2v12c0 1.1-.9 2-2 2H4c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V6c0-1.1.9-2 2-2z"></path><polyline points="22,6 12,13 2,6"></polyline></svg>`;

    <script>
        const registry = ${JSON.stringify(historicalCases)};

        // UTF-8 safe base64 helpers for GitHub API (handles Unicode like · in history_registry.json)
        function utf8ToBase64(str) {
            const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(str);
            let binary = '';
            for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) binary += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);
            return btoa(binary);
        }
        function base64ToUtf8(b64) {
            const binary = atob(b64.replace(/\s/g, ''));
            const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
            for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
            return new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);
        }

        // Large file to Base64 helper (chunked to avoid call-stack limits on 45-70 MB videos)
        async function fileToBase64(file) {
            const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
            const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
            const chunkSize = 0x8000; // 32 KB chunks
            let binary = '';
            for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunkSize) {
                binary += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, bytes.subarray(i, i + chunkSize));
            }
            return btoa(binary);
        }

        // YouTube URL helpers
        function extractYouTubeId(url) {
            if (!url) return null;
            try {
                const parsed = new URL(url.trim());
                const host = parsed.hostname.replace(/^www\./, '');
                let id = null;
                if (host === 'youtu.be') {
                    id = parsed.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean)[0] || null;
                } else if (host === 'youtube.com' || host === 'youtube-nocookie.com') {
                    if (parsed.pathname === '/watch') {
                        id = parsed.searchParams.get('v');
                    } else if (parsed.pathname.startsWith('/embed/') || parsed.pathname.startsWith('/shorts/')) {
                        id = parsed.pathname.split('/')[2] || null;
                    }
                }
                return id && /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{11}$/.test(id) ? id : null;
            } catch (e) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        function isYouTubeUrl(url) {
            return extractYouTubeId(url) !== null;
        }

        // =========================================================================
        // THEATER MODE MODAL LOGIC
        // =========================================================================
        const theaterModal = document.getElementById('theater-modal');
        const modalTitle = document.getElementById('modal-title');
        const modalMedia = document.getElementById('modal-media');
        const modalClose = document.getElementById('modal-close');
        let pausedInlineVideo = null;

function openTheaterModal(title, mediaElement, isVideo, isYouTube, ytId) {
            modalTitle.textContent = title;
            modalMedia.innerHTML = '';

            // Pause any playing inline video in the card
            if (isVideo && mediaElement.tagName === 'VIDEO' && !mediaElement.paused) {
                pausedInlineVideo = mediaElement;
                mediaElement.pause();
            }

            if (isYouTube) {
                const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
                iframe.src = \`https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/\${ytId}?autoplay=1&rel=0\`;
                iframe.className = 'w-[90vw] max-w-5xl aspect-video rounded-xl border border-slate-700';
                iframe.allow = 'autoplay; fullscreen; picture-in-picture';
                iframe.allowFullscreen = true;
                iframe.title = title;
                modalMedia.appendChild(iframe);
            } else if (isVideo) {
                const video = document.createElement('video');
                video.src = mediaElement.src;
                if (mediaElement.poster) video.poster = mediaElement.poster;
                video.controls = true;
                video.autoplay = true;
                video.playsinline = true;
                video.className = 'max-h-[85vh] max-w-[92vw] rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-black';
                modalMedia.appendChild(video);
            } else {
                const img = document.createElement('img');
                img.src = mediaElement.src;
                img.alt = title + ' Walkthrough';
                img.className = 'max-h-[85vh] max-w-[92vw] object-contain rounded-xl border border-slate-700';
                modalMedia.appendChild(img);
            }

            theaterModal.classList.remove('hidden');
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        }

        function closeTheaterModal() {
            // Pause and clear modal video
            const modalVideo = modalMedia.querySelector('video');
            if (modalVideo) {
                modalVideo.pause();
                modalVideo.src = '';
            }
            modalMedia.innerHTML = '';
            theaterModal.classList.add('hidden');
            document.body.style.overflow = '';
            
            // Resume inline video if it was playing
            if (pausedInlineVideo) {
                pausedInlineVideo.play().catch(() => {});
                pausedInlineVideo = null;
            }
        }

        modalClose.addEventListener('click', closeTheaterModal);
        theaterModal.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (e.target === theaterModal) closeTheaterModal();
        });
        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            if (e.key === 'Escape' && !theaterModal.classList.contains('hidden')) {
                closeTheaterModal();
            }
        });

        // =========================================================================
        // ADMIN UPLOAD MODAL LOGIC
        // =========================================================================
        const adminModal = document.getElementById('admin-upload-modal');
        const adminModalClose = document.getElementById('admin-modal-close');
        const ghTokenInput = document.getElementById('gh-token-input');
        const clearTokenBtn = document.getElementById('clear-token-btn');
        const mediaFileInput = document.getElementById('media-file-input');
        const thumbnailPreview = document.getElementById('thumbnail-preview');
        const thumbnailImg = document.getElementById('thumbnail-img');
        const projectTitleInput = document.getElementById('project-title-input');
        const projectSubtitleInput = document.getElementById('project-subtitle-input');
        const projectReqIdInput = document.getElementById('project-reqid-input');
        const publishBtn = document.getElementById('publish-btn');
        const statusStep = document.getElementById('status-step');
        const statusLog = document.getElementById('status-log');
        let headerClickCount = 0;
        let headerClickTimer = null;

        // Load saved token
        const savedToken = localStorage.getItem('gh_portfolio_pat');
        if (savedToken) {
            ghTokenInput.value = savedToken;
            validateToken(savedToken);
        }

        function addStatusLog(message, isError = false) {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = isError ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-slate-300';
            div.textContent = \`[\${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}] \${message}\`;
            statusLog.appendChild(div);
            statusLog.scrollTop = statusLog.scrollHeight;
        }

        function setStatusStep(step) {
            statusStep.textContent = step;
        }

        function validateToken(token) {
            if (token && (token.startsWith('github_pat_') || token.startsWith('ghp_') || token.length >= 20)) {
                publishBtn.disabled = false;
                addStatusLog('Token loaded ✓');
            } else {
                publishBtn.disabled = true;
            }
        }

        ghTokenInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            const token = e.target.value.trim();
            if (token) {
                localStorage.setItem('gh_portfolio_pat', token);
            } else {
                localStorage.removeItem('gh_portfolio_pat');
            }
            validateToken(token);
        });

        clearTokenBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            ghTokenInput.value = '';
            localStorage.removeItem('gh_portfolio_pat');
            publishBtn.disabled = true;
            addStatusLog('Token cleared');
        });

        // Thumbnail generation for video files
        mediaFileInput.addEventListener('change', async (e) => {
            const file = e.target.files[0];
            if (!file) return;

            thumbnailPreview.classList.add('hidden');
            thumbnailImg.src = '';

            if (file.type.startsWith('video/')) {
                addStatusLog('Analyzing video for best thumbnail...');
                setStatusStep('Generating thumbnail...');
                
                try {
                    const thumbnailDataUrl = await generateSmartThumbnail(file);
                    thumbnailImg.src = thumbnailDataUrl;
                    thumbnailPreview.classList.remove('hidden');
                    addStatusLog('Smart thumbnail generated ✓');
                    setStatusStep('Ready to publish');
                } catch (err) {
                    addStatusLog('Thumbnail generation failed: ' + err.message, true);
                    setStatusStep('Error');
                }
            } else if (file.type === 'image/gif') {
                // For GIFs, use first frame as poster via canvas
                const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
                const video = document.createElement('video');
                video.src = url;
                video.muted = true;
                video.preload = 'metadata';
                
                await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                    video.onloadeddata = () => {
                        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
                        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
                        canvas.width = Math.min(1280, video.videoWidth);
                        canvas.height = canvas.width * (video.videoHeight / video.videoWidth);
                        ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
                        thumbnailImg.src = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.85);
                        thumbnailPreview.classList.remove('hidden');
                        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
                        addStatusLog('GIF thumbnail generated ✓');
                        setStatusStep('Ready to publish');
                        resolve();
                    };
                    video.onerror = () => {
                        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
                        reject(new Error('Failed to load GIF'));
                    };
                });
            }
        });

        async function generateSmartThumbnail(file) {
            return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
                const video = document.createElement('video');
                video.src = url;
                video.muted = true;
                video.preload = 'metadata';
                video.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';

                video.onloadedmetadata = async () => {
                    const duration = video.duration;
                    if (!duration || duration < 1) {
                        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
                        reject(new Error('Video too short'));
                        return;
                    }

                    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
                    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
                    const targetWidth = 1280;
                    
                    // Sample 5 timestamps: 20%, 35%, 50%, 65%, 80%
                    const timestamps = [0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65, 0.8].map(t => duration * t);
                    let bestFrame = null;
                    let bestScore = -1;

                    for (const ts of timestamps) {
                        await new Promise((res, rej) => {
                            video.onseeked = () => res();
                            video.onerror = () => rej(new Error('Seek failed'));
                            video.currentTime = ts;
                        });

                        // Draw frame to canvas
                        canvas.width = targetWidth;
                        canvas.height = targetWidth * (video.videoHeight / video.videoWidth);
                        ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

                        // Compute grayscale standard deviation
                        const imageData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
                        const data = imageData.data;
                        let sum = 0;
                        let sumSq = 0;
                        let pixelCount = 0;

                        for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i += 4) {
                            const r = data[i];
                            const g = data[i + 1];
                            const b = data[i + 2];
                            const gray = 0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b;
                            sum += gray;
                            sumSq += gray * gray;
                            pixelCount++;
                        }

                        const mean = sum / pixelCount;
                        const variance = (sumSq / pixelCount) - (mean * mean);
                        const stdDev = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, variance));

                        // Reject blank/black frames (stdDev < 18)
                        if (stdDev >= 18 && stdDev > bestScore) {
                            bestScore = stdDev;
                            bestFrame = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.85);
                        }
                    }

                    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

                    if (bestFrame) {
                        resolve(bestFrame);
                    } else {
                        // Fallback: use middle frame even if low contrast
                        video.currentTime = duration * 0.5;
                        await new Promise(res => { video.onseeked = res; });
                        canvas.width = targetWidth;
                        canvas.height = targetWidth * (video.videoHeight / video.videoWidth);
                        ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
                        resolve(canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.85));
                    }
                };

                video.onerror = () => {
                    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
                    reject(new Error('Video load failed'));
                };
            });
        }

        function generateSlug(text) {
            return text
                .toLowerCase()
                .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
                .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '')
                .substring(0, 80);
        }

        function getFileExtension(filename) {
            return filename.split('.').pop().toLowerCase();
        }

        async function githubApiRequest(endpoint, options = {}) {
            const token = ghTokenInput.value.trim();
            if (!token) throw new Error('No GitHub token provided');

            const headers = {
                'Authorization': \`Bearer \${token}\`,
                'Accept': 'application/vnd.github+json',
                'X-GitHub-Api-Version': '2022-11-28',
                ...options.headers
            };

            const response = await fetch(\`https://api.github.com\${endpoint}\`, {
                ...options,
                headers
            });

            if (!response.ok) {
                const errorText = await response.text();
                throw new Error(\`GitHub API error (\${response.status}): \${errorText}\`);
            }

            return response.json();
        }

        async function uploadToGitHubContents(filePath, content, message, sha = null, isAlreadyBase64 = false) {
            const endpoint = \`/repos/parth33320/enterprise-portfolio/contents/\${filePath}\`;
            const body = {
                message,
                content: isAlreadyBase64 ? content : utf8ToBase64(content),
                ...(sha && { sha })
            };
            return githubApiRequest(endpoint, {
                method: 'PUT',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify(body)
            });
        }

        async function getFileSha(filePath) {
            try {
                const data = await githubApiRequest(\`/repos/parth33320/enterprise-portfolio/contents/\${filePath}\`);
                return data.sha;
            } catch (e) {
                return null; // File doesn't exist
            }
        }

        publishBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            const token = ghTokenInput.value.trim();
            const file = mediaFileInput.files[0];
            const title = projectTitleInput.value.trim();
            const subtitle = projectSubtitleInput.value.trim();
            const reqId = projectReqIdInput.value.trim();
            const ytUrl = youtubeUrlInput.value.trim();

            if (!token) {
                addStatusLog('Error: GitHub token required', true);
                return;
            }
            const hasFile = !!file;
            const hasYouTube = !!ytUrl && isYouTubeUrl(ytUrl);
            
            if (!hasFile && !hasYouTube) {
                addStatusLog('Error: Provide either a file or a YouTube URL', true);
                return;
            }
            if (hasFile && hasYouTube) {
                addStatusLog('Error: Provide either a file OR a YouTube URL, not both', true);
                return;
            }
            if (hasYouTube) {
                const ytId = extractYouTubeId(ytUrl);
                if (!ytId) {
                    addStatusLog('Error: Invalid YouTube URL format', true);
                    return;
                }
            }
            if (!title) {
                addStatusLog('Error: Project title required', true);
                return;
            }

            publishBtn.disabled = true;
            statusLog.innerHTML = '';

            try {
                if (hasYouTube) {
                    // YouTube-only publish path
                    setStatusStep('1/2 Updating registry (YouTube)...');
                    addStatusLog('Publishing YouTube video...');

                    const registryData = await githubApiRequest('/repos/parth33320/enterprise-portfolio/contents/_job_queue/history_registry.json');
                    const currentRegistry = JSON.parse(base64ToUtf8(registryData.content));

                    const newEntry = {
                        companyName: title,
                        sector: 'Custom Upload',
                        jobTitle: subtitle || '',
                        reqId: reqId || '',
                        painPoint: 'Custom uploaded demonstration.',
                        solution: 'Published via portfolio admin modal (YouTube).',
                        beforeCost: 0,
                        afterCosts: 0,
                        investmentCost: 0,
                        cpi: 1.0,
                        spi: 1.0,
                        piPredictability: 100,
                        gifAsset: ytUrl,  // Store raw YouTube URL
                        // No posterAsset for YouTube
                        implementationSchedule: 'N/A'
                    };

                    const updatedRegistry = [newEntry, ...currentRegistry];
                    await uploadToGitHubContents(
                        '_job_queue/history_registry.json',
                        JSON.stringify(updatedRegistry, null, 2),
                        \`Add demo entry: \${title}\`,
                        registryData.sha
                    );
                    addStatusLog('Registry updated ✓');

                    setStatusStep('2/2 Done!');
                    addStatusLog('Success! GitHub Actions is deploying your live site (~60s).');
                    addStatusLog('Refresh this page in a minute to see your new demo at the top.');

                    // Reset form
                    mediaFileInput.value = '';
                    youtubeUrlInput.value = '';
                    projectTitleInput.value = '';
                    projectSubtitleInput.value = '';
                    projectReqIdInput.value = '';
                    thumbnailPreview.classList.add('hidden');
                    thumbnailImg.src = '';

                } else {
                    // File upload pipeline
                    setStatusStep('1/4 Generating smart thumbnail...');
                    addStatusLog('Starting publish pipeline...');

                    const slug = generateSlug(reqId || title);
                    const ext = getFileExtension(file.name);
                    const mediaFileName = \`\${slug}.\${ext}\`;
                    const posterFileName = \`\${slug}-poster.jpg\`;
                    let mediaPath, posterPath;

                    // Step 2: Upload media (always via Contents API; 70 MB raw ≈ 93 MB Base64, under 100 MB limit)
                    const MAX_CONTENTS_API_SIZE = 70 * 1024 * 1024; // 70 MB raw = ~93 MB Base64 payload
                    const isTooLarge = file.size >= MAX_CONTENTS_API_SIZE;

                    if (isTooLarge) {
                        const mb = (file.size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1);
                        throw new Error(\`File is \${mb} MB — GitHub Contents API limit is 100 MB (Base64 expands ~33%). Please compress your MP4 below 70 MB raw or upload via GitHub Releases UI manually.\`);
                    }

                    setStatusStep('2/4 Uploading media...');
                    addStatusLog(\`Uploading \${mediaFileName} (\${(file.size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2)} MB)...\`);

                    const base64Content = await fileToBase64(file);
                    const sha = await getFileSha(\`docs/assets/\${mediaFileName}\`);
                    await uploadToGitHubContents(
                        \`docs/assets/\${mediaFileName}\`,
                        base64Content,
                        \`Add demo media: \${title}\`,
                        sha,
                        true
                    );
                    mediaPath = \`docs/assets/\${mediaFileName}\`;
                    addStatusLog('Repo upload complete ✓');

                    // Step 3: Upload poster
                    setStatusStep('3/4 Uploading thumbnail...');
                    addStatusLog('Uploading poster thumbnail...');

                    // Convert thumbnail data URL to base64
                    const posterBase64 = thumbnailImg.src.split(',')[1];
                    if (!posterBase64) {
                        throw new Error('No thumbnail generated');
                    }

                    const posterSha = await getFileSha(\`docs/assets/\${posterFileName}\`);
                    await uploadToGitHubContents(
                        \`docs/assets/\${posterFileName}\`,
                        posterBase64,
                        \`Add demo poster: \${title}\`,
                        posterSha,
                        true
                    );
                    posterPath = \`docs/assets/\${posterFileName}\`;
                    addStatusLog('Poster upload complete ✓');

                    // Step 4: Update registry
                    setStatusStep('4/4 Updating registry...');
                    addStatusLog('Updating history_registry.json...');

                    const registryData = await githubApiRequest('/repos/parth33320/enterprise-portfolio/contents/_job_queue/history_registry.json');
                    const currentRegistry = JSON.parse(base64ToUtf8(registryData.content));
                    
                    const newEntry = {
                        companyName: title,
                        sector: 'Custom Upload',
                        jobTitle: subtitle || '',
                        reqId: reqId || '',
                        painPoint: 'Custom uploaded demonstration.',
                        solution: 'Published via portfolio admin modal.',
                        beforeCost: 0,
                        afterCosts: 0,
                        investmentCost: 0,
                        cpi: 1.0,
                        spi: 1.0,
                        piPredictability: 100,
                        gifAsset: mediaPath,
                        posterAsset: posterPath,
                        implementationSchedule: 'N/A'
                    };

                    const updatedRegistry = [newEntry, ...currentRegistry];
                    await uploadToGitHubContents(
                        '_job_queue/history_registry.json',
                        JSON.stringify(updatedRegistry, null, 2),
                        \`Add demo entry: \${title}\`,
                        registryData.sha
                    );
                    addStatusLog('Registry updated ✓');

                    setStatusStep('4/4 Done!');
                    addStatusLog('Success! GitHub Actions is deploying your live site (~60s).');
                    addStatusLog('Refresh this page in a minute to see your new demo at the top.');

                    // Reset form
                    mediaFileInput.value = '';
                    youtubeUrlInput.value = '';
                    projectTitleInput.value = '';
                    projectSubtitleInput.value = '';
                    projectReqIdInput.value = '';
                    thumbnailPreview.classList.add('hidden');
                    thumbnailImg.src = '';
                }

            } catch (err) {
                addStatusLog('Error: ' + err.message, true);
                setStatusStep('Error');
                console.error(err);
            } finally {
                publishBtn.disabled = false;
            }
        });

        // Modal open/close
        function openAdminModal() {
            adminModal.classList.remove('hidden');
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
            mediaFileInput.focus();
        }

        function closeAdminModal() {
            adminModal.classList.add('hidden');
            document.body.style.overflow = '';
        }

        adminModalClose.addEventListener('click', closeAdminModal);
        adminModal.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (e.target === adminModal) closeAdminModal();
        });

        // Trigger 1: Ctrl+Shift+U
        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.shiftKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'u') {
                e.preventDefault();
                openAdminModal();
            }
        });

        // Trigger 2: Type "xjupload" in search
        const searchInput = document.getElementById('archive-search');
        searchInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            if (e.target.value.toLowerCase() === 'xjupload') {
                e.target.value = '';
                executeFilter(false);
                openAdminModal();
            }
        });

        // Trigger 3: 5 clicks on "Parth Patel" in header within 2 seconds
        const headerName = document.getElementById('github-link'); // This is the GitHub link, but we need the name element
        // We'll add a click handler to the name text specifically
        document.querySelector('h1')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
            headerClickCount++;
            if (headerClickCount === 1) {
                headerClickTimer = setTimeout(() => { headerClickCount = 0; }, 2000);
            }
            if (headerClickCount >= 5) {
                clearTimeout(headerClickTimer);
                headerClickCount = 0;
                openAdminModal();
            }
        });

        // =========================================================================
        // PORTFOLIO RENDERING
        // =========================================================================
        function isVideoUrl(url) {
            if (!url) return false;
            const cleanUrl = url.split('?')[0].toLowerCase();
            return cleanUrl.endsWith('.mp4') || cleanUrl.endsWith('.webm');
        }

        function getMediaSource(item) {
            return item.mediaAsset || item.gifAsset || '';
        }

        function getYouTubeId(item) {
            const src = getMediaSource(item);
            return extractYouTubeId(src);
        }

        function renderPortfolio() {
            const grid = document.getElementById('portfolio-grid');
            if (!registry || registry.length === 0) {
                grid.innerHTML = '<div class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono p-8 text-center bg-slate-900/40 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800">No walkthrough entries recorded in system index.</div>';
                return;
            }

            grid.innerHTML = registry.map(item => {
                const reqBadge = item.reqId ? \`<span class="text-slate-400 font-mono text-xs">REQ ID: <span class="text-slate-200 font-bold">\${item.reqId}</span></span>\` : '';
                const hasJobTitle = item.jobTitle && item.jobTitle.trim() !== '';
                const mediaSrc = getMediaSource(item);
                const ytId = getYouTubeId(item);
                const isVideo = !ytId && isVideoUrl(mediaSrc);
                const isYouTube = !!ytId;
                const posterSrc = item.posterAsset || '';

                const mediaHtml = isYouTube
                    ? \`<iframe src="https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/\${ytId}?rel=0" title="\${item.companyName}" class="w-full h-full border-0" loading="lazy" allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share" allowfullscreen data-theater-youtube="\${ytId}"></iframe>\`
                    : isVideo
                        ? \`<video src="\${mediaSrc}" poster="\${posterSrc}" controls playsinline preload="none" class="w-full h-full object-contain bg-black" data-theater-video="true"></video>\`
                        : \`<img src="\${mediaSrc}" loading="lazy" decoding="async" class="w-full h-full object-cover cursor-zoom-in" alt="\${item.companyName} Walkthrough" onerror="this.style.display='none'; this.nextElementSibling.style.display='flex';" data-theater-image="true">\`;

                return \`
                    <div class="case-card bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800 hover:border-cyan-500/40 p-6 rounded-2xl space-y-5 shadow-2xl transition-all" data-search="\${item.companyName} \${item.jobTitle || ''} \${item.reqId || ''}">

                        <!-- Clean Job Posting Header Strip -->
                        <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center border-b border-slate-800/80 pb-4 gap-2">
                            <div>
                                <div class="text-cyan-400 font-mono text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">\${item.companyName}</div>
                                \${hasJobTitle ? \`<h2 class="text-lg font-extrabold text-white font-sans mt-0.5 tracking-tight">\${item.jobTitle}</h2>\` : ''}
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                \${reqBadge}
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- Walkthrough Media Container -->
                        <div class="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl overflow-hidden aspect-video w-full flex items-center justify-center shadow-inner relative group">
                            \${mediaHtml}
                            <div class="hidden absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center text-xs text-slate-500 bg-slate-950 font-mono">
                                [Walkthrough media generating in build pipeline]
                            </div>
                            <!-- Expand Button -->
                            <button class="absolute top-3 right-3 bg-slate-900/80 hover:bg-slate-800/90 text-slate-300 hover:text-white p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-700/50 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity z-10" aria-label="Theater Mode" onclick="openTheaterModal('\${item.companyName}\${hasJobTitle ? ' - ' + item.jobTitle : ''}', this.closest('.aspect-video').querySelector('video, img, iframe'), \${isVideo}, \${isYouTube}, '\${ytId || ''}')">
                                ${expandIcon}
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>\`;
            }).join('');

            // Add click handlers for theater mode on images (direct click)
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-theater-image="true"]').forEach(img => {
                img.style.cursor = 'zoom-in';
                img.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                    const card = e.target.closest('.case-card');
                    const title = card.querySelector('.text-cyan-400').textContent + (card.querySelector('h2') ? ' - ' + card.querySelector('h2').textContent : '');
                    openTheaterModal(title, e.target, false);
                });
            });

            // Add click handlers for theater mode on YouTube iframes (direct click)
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-theater-youtube]').forEach(iframe => {
                iframe.style.cursor = 'zoom-in';
                iframe.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                    const card = e.target.closest('.case-card');
                    const title = card.querySelector('.text-cyan-400').textContent + (card.querySelector('h2') ? ' - ' + card.querySelector('h2').textContent : '');
                    const ytId = e.target.dataset.theaterYoutube;
                    openTheaterModal(title, e.target, false, true, ytId);
                });
            });
        }

        function executeFilter(shouldJump = false) {
            const term = document.getElementById('archive-search').value.toLowerCase().trim();
            const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.case-card');
            let visibleCount = 0;
            let firstVisibleCard = null;

            cards.forEach(card => {
                const haystack = card.getAttribute('data-search').toLowerCase();
                const isMatch = haystack.includes(term);
                card.style.display = isMatch ? 'block' : 'none';
                if (isMatch) {
                    visibleCount++;
                    if (!firstVisibleCard) firstVisibleCard = card;
                }
            });

            // Handle Empty State
            let emptyStateEl = document.getElementById('search-empty-state');
            if (visibleCount === 0) {
                if (!emptyStateEl) {
                    emptyStateEl = document.createElement('div');
                    emptyStateEl.id = 'search-empty-state';
                    emptyStateEl.className = 'bg-slate-900/40 border border-dashed border-slate-800 p-12 rounded-2xl text-center space-y-3';
                    document.getElementById('portfolio-grid').appendChild(emptyStateEl);
                }
                emptyStateEl.innerHTML = \`
                    <div class="text-cyan-400 font-mono text-2xl font-bold">🔍</div>
                    <div class="text-sm font-bold text-slate-200">No Business Solutions Found</div>
                    <div class="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">No matching walkthrough entries found for "<span class="text-cyan-400">\${term}</span>". Try searching company name, job title, or REQ ID.</div>
                \`;
                emptyStateEl.style.display = 'block';
            } else if (emptyStateEl) {
                emptyStateEl.style.display = 'none';
            }

            // Smooth Jump to Top Match if requested or on Enter
            if (shouldJump && firstVisibleCard) {
                firstVisibleCard.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
            }
        }

        function handleSearchKeydown(event) {
            if (event.key === 'Enter') {
                executeFilter(true);
            }
        }

        renderPortfolio();
    </script>
</body>
</html>
`;

fs.writeFileSync(path.join(publicDir, 'index.html'), htmlContent);
console.log('[Factory] Portfolio website successfully compiled with job-centric walkthrough layout.');

=== scripts/frame_to_gif.py ===
import sys
import os
import glob
from PIL import Image

def convert_frames_to_gif(frame_dir, output_gif_path, duration_per_frame_ms=500):
    png_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(frame_dir, "*.png")))
    if not png_files:
        print(f"[GIF Compiler] No PNG frames found in {frame_dir}")
        sys.exit(1)

    print(f"[GIF Compiler] Compiling {len(png_files)} PNG frames into GIF...")
    images = [Image.open(f) for f in png_files]

    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(output_gif_path), exist_ok=True)
    images[0].save(
        output_gif_path,
        save_all=True,
        append_images=images[1:],
        optimize=True,
        duration=duration_per_frame_ms,
        loop=0
    )
    print(f"[GIF Compiler] Successfully saved optimized GIF loop at: {output_gif_path}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) < 3:
        print("Usage: python frame_to_gif.py <frame_dir> <output_gif_path> [duration_ms]")
        sys.exit(1)

    f_dir = sys.argv[1]
    out_path = sys.argv[2]
    dur = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 500
    convert_frames_to_gif(f_dir, out_path, dur)


=== scripts/generate-synthetic-data.js ===
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

function generateDomainData(industrySector, count = 100000) {
  console.log(`[Big Data Simulation] Synthesizing ${count} rows of enterprise telemetry for: ${industrySector}`);
  
  const dataset = [];
  const anomalies = ['OUT_OF_BOUNDS_LATENCY', 'NULL_PO_POINTER', 'LEAN_SIGMA_MUDA_DETECTED', 'CONCURRENCY_LOCK_FAIL', 'NONE'];
  let varianceSeed = industrySector.length;

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    varianceSeed = (varianceSeed * 9301 + 49297) % 233280;
    const pseudoRandom = varianceSeed / 233280;
    
    // Model complex edge cases & operational faults under heavy loads
    const anomalyType = i % 450 === 0 ? anomalies[Math.floor(pseudoRandom * (anomalies.length - 1))] : 'NONE';
    const baseMetric = pseudoRandom * 1200;
    
    dataset.push({
      transactionId: `TX-${1000000 + i}`,
      timestamp: new Date(Date.now() - (i * 60000)).toISOString(),
      operationalCost: parseFloat((baseMetric * (anomalyType !== 'NONE' ? 4.2 : 1.0)).toFixed(2)),
      processingTimeSeconds: parseFloat((pseudoRandom * 90 * (anomalyType !== 'NONE' ? 6.5 : 1.0)).toFixed(2)),
      anomalyStatus: anomalyType
    });
  }
  
  const publicDir = path.join(__dirname, '../public');
  if (!fs.existsSync(publicDir)) fs.mkdirSync(publicDir);
  
  // Persist a clean sample slice for frontend UI review
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(publicDir, 'telemetry_sample.json'), JSON.stringify(dataset.slice(0, 300), null, 2));
  console.log(`[Big Data Simulation] Successfully verified and saved dataset metrics.`);
  return dataset;
}

module.exports = { generateDomainData };


=== scripts/generate-walkthrough-gif.js ===
const { chromium } = require('playwright');
const { execSync } = require('child_process');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

async function runRecordingPipeline() {
  console.log('[Jules Media Engine] Initializing domain-agnostic headless recording workspace...');
  
  const queueDir = path.join(__dirname, '../_job_queue');
  const jobFiles = fs.readdirSync(queueDir).filter(f => f.endsWith('.json') && f !== 'history_registry.json' && f !== 'schema.json');
  const historyPath = path.join(queueDir, 'history_registry.json');
  
  let jobData;
  if (jobFiles.length > 0) {
    jobData = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(queueDir, jobFiles[0]), 'utf8'));
  } else if (fs.existsSync(historyPath)) {
    const history = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(historyPath, 'utf8'));
    if (history.length > 0) {
      jobData = history[0];
    }
  }

  if (!jobData) {
    console.log('[Jules Media Engine] No job data found. Exiting media compiler.');
    return;
  }
  const targetReqId = jobData.reqId.toLowerCase();
  const targetGifName = `${targetReqId}.gif`;

  const beforeCost = jobData.beforeCost || 2100000;
  const afterCosts = jobData.afterCosts || 420000;
  const netSavings = beforeCost - afterCosts;
  const roi = (((netSavings) / jobData.investmentCost) * 100).toFixed(2);

  const frameStagingDir = path.join(__dirname, '../_frame_staging');
  if (fs.existsSync(frameStagingDir)) {
    fs.rmSync(frameStagingDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
  fs.mkdirSync(frameStagingDir, { recursive: true });

  const outputGifDocsPath = path.join(__dirname, `../docs/assets/${targetGifName}`);

  const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true });
  const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1280, height: 720 } });
  
  let frameIdx = 1000;
  const captureFrames = async (count, delayMs = 600) => {
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const framePath = path.join(frameStagingDir, `frame_${frameIdx++}.png`);
      await page.screenshot({ path: framePath });
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, delayMs));
    }
  };

  const renderTitleCard = (sectionNum, title, body) => `
    <html lang="en">
    <head>
      <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;600;700;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    </head>
    <body style="background-color: #020617; color: #f8fafc; font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; height: 100vh; margin: 0; padding: 40px; box-sizing: border-box; -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased; overflow: hidden;">
      <div style="width: 100%; max-width: 900px; border: 1px solid #1e293b; padding: 40px; border-radius: 20px; background-color: #0f172a; box-shadow: 0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0,0,0,0.6);">
        <span style="font-family: monospace; color: #22d3ee; font-size: 11px; font-weight: bold; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.12em;">${sectionNum}</span>
        <h1 style="font-size: 24px; font-weight: 800; color: #ffffff; margin-top: 8px; margin-bottom: 20px; letter-spacing: -0.02em;">${title}</h1>
        <div style="font-size: 13px; color: #94a3b8; line-height: 1.7;">${body}</div>
      </div>
    </body>
    </html>`;

  // Helper to simulate smooth cursor movement & click ripple animation on a button
  const simulateClickWithRipple = async (selector) => {
    const box = await page.locator(selector).boundingBox();
    if (box) {
      const clickX = box.x + box.width / 2;
      const clickY = box.y + box.height / 2;
      await page.evaluate(({ x, y }) => {
        if (typeof window.triggerRippleAt === 'function') {
          window.triggerRippleAt(x, y);
        }
      }, { x: clickX, y: clickY });
      await captureFrames(2, 300);
    }
    await page.click(selector);
  };

  // =========================================================================
  // --- SECTION 1 OF 5: EXECUTIVE BRIEF & TARGET METRICS ---
  // =========================================================================
  const section1Summary = `
    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; border-bottom: 1px solid #1e293b; padding-bottom: 14px; margin-bottom: 20px;">
      <div>
        <div style="font-size: 12px; font-weight: bold; color: #38bdf8; font-family: monospace; text-transform: uppercase;">WonderBotz Client Automation Portfolio Optimization</div>
        <div style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: 800; color: #ffffff;">UiPath REFramework, Document Understanding, and Action Center Platform</div>
      </div>
      <div style="background-color: #052e16; border: 1px solid #166534; color: #4ade80; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; font-weight: bold; padding: 6px 14px; border-radius: 9999px;">
        +${roi}% ROI
      </div>
    </div>
    <div style="font-size: 13px; color: #94a3b8; line-height: 1.6;">
      Automating unattended bot exception recovery, vendor PDF invoice parsing, and manager 1-click approvals across client automation portfolios.
    </div>`;
  
  // Section 1 Title Card (Clean title card - NO KPI CHIPS rendered here)
  await page.setContent(renderTitleCard("SECTION 1 OF 5 | EXECUTIVE BRIEF", "WonderBotz Technical Account Manager (UiPath) Executive Brief", section1Summary));
  await captureFrames(8, 600);

  // Reload application workspace Section 1 view (KPI Chips Grid renders exclusively inside this view)
  await page.goto('http://localhost:3000/demo?mode=section1');
  await captureFrames(10, 650);

  // =========================================================================
  // --- SECTION 2 OF 5: TECHNICAL DEEP DIVE & ARCHITECTURE DIAGRAM ---
  // =========================================================================
  const section2Explanation = `
    <strong>Comparative System Architecture & Information Flow:</strong><br><br>
    • <strong>Legacy Architecture:</strong> Unattended bot crashes force manual text log searching; unparsed vendor PDF invoices require manual typing, causing 48+ hour SLA delays.<br>
    • <strong>Optimized Architecture:</strong> UiPath REFramework handles bot health & error recovery in <strong><20ms</strong>, Document AI parses PDF invoices, and Action Center dispatches 1-click approvals.`;

  await page.setContent(renderTitleCard("SECTION 2 OF 5 | SYSTEM ARCHITECTURE & DEEP DIVE", "Comparative System Architecture & Information Flow", section2Explanation));
  await captureFrames(8, 600);

  // Reload application workspace Section 2 view
  await page.goto('http://localhost:3000/demo?mode=section2');
  await captureFrames(10, 650);

  // =========================================================================
  // --- SECTION 3 OF 5: CONSOLIDATED OPTIMIZED WORKFLOW SIMULATION ---
  // =========================================================================
  const section3Explanation = `
    <strong>Consolidated UiPath Platform & Architectural Justification (Process of Elimination):</strong><br><br>
    • <strong>UiPath REFramework + Document AI + Action Center Selected:</strong> Combines automated bot health checks, machine learning PDF invoice parsing, and 1-click human approvals.<br>
    • <strong>Elimination Reason vs Custom Scripts:</strong> Custom Python scripts lack centralized Orchestrator cloud dashboards, automated retries, enterprise audit logs, and SLA tracking.<br>
    • <strong>Elimination Reason vs Pure LLMs:</strong> Standard LLMs can make mathematical errors on financial numbers. REFramework guarantees 100% deterministic execution.`;

  // Section 3 Title Card (Contains Process of Elimination Architectural Justification)
  await page.setContent(renderTitleCard("SECTION 3 OF 5 | OPTIMIZED WORKFLOW", "Consolidated UiPath Platform & Architectural Justification", section3Explanation));
  await captureFrames(8, 600);

  // Navigate to application workspace Section 3 Consolidated View
  await page.goto('http://localhost:3000/demo?mode=section3');
  await captureFrames(3, 500);

  // Run Consolidated UiPath Workflow Execution
  await simulateClickWithRipple('#btn-run-uipath-workflow');
  await captureFrames(14, 650);

  // =========================================================================
  // --- SECTION 4 OF 5: STANDALONE APPENDIX & KEY TERMS GUIDE ---
  // =========================================================================
  const section4Explanation = `
    <strong>Web Services Glossary & Solution Choice Rationale:</strong><br><br>
    • Dedicated standalone reference screen defining technical terms (REFramework, Document Understanding, Action Center, Orchestrator).<br>
    • Provides clear civilian accessibility for non-technical stakeholders and hiring managers.`;

  await page.setContent(renderTitleCard("SECTION 4 OF 5 | APPENDIX & KEY TERMS GUIDE", "UiPath Automation Terminology & Solution Choice Rationale Appendix", section4Explanation));
  await captureFrames(8, 600);

  // Reload application workspace Section 4 Appendix view
  await page.goto('http://localhost:3000/demo?mode=appendix');
  await captureFrames(12, 650);

  // =========================================================================
  // --- SECTION 5 OF 5: DETAILED INNER SOFTWARE WORKINGS (PART 1 & PART 2) ---
  // =========================================================================
  await page.goto('http://localhost:3000/demo?mode=techdetails1');
  await captureFrames(10, 650);

  await page.goto('http://localhost:3000/demo?mode=techdetails2');
  await captureFrames(10, 650);

  await browser.close();

  console.log('[Jules Media Engine] Compiling PNG frames into GIF using Pillow compiler...');

  [path.dirname(outputGifDocsPath)].forEach(dir => {
    if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  });

  try {
    execSync(`python3 scripts/frame_to_gif.py "${frameStagingDir}" "${outputGifDocsPath}" 600`);
    console.log(`[Jules Media Engine] Walkthrough GIF loop successfully generated at:\n - ${outputGifDocsPath}`);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[Jules Media Engine] Media pipeline compilation error:', err.message);
  }
}

runRecordingPipeline();

const http = require('http');
const url = require('url');

const PORT = 3000;

function generateHtml(mode) {
  const isSection1 = mode === 'section1';
  const isSection2 = mode === 'section2';
  const isSection3 = mode === 'section3' || mode === 'section4'; // Consolidated Optimized Solution view
  const isSection4 = mode === 'appendix' || mode === 'section5';
  const isSection5 = mode === 'techdetails1' || mode === 'techdetails2' || mode === 'section6';
  const techPart2 = mode === 'techdetails2';

  return `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>WonderBotz UiPath Client Services Workspace</title>
  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
  <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;600;700&family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
  <style>
    body { font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif; }
    .font-mono { font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace; }

    /* Virtual Cursor & Ripple Effect Animations */
    #virtual-cursor {
      position: fixed;
      width: 20px;
      height: 20px;
      pointer-events: none;
      z-index: 10000;
      transition: all 0.25s ease-out;
      transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
      display: none;
    }
    .click-ripple {
      position: fixed;
      width: 48px;
      height: 48px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      background: rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.4);
      border: 2px solid #22d3ee;
      pointer-events: none;
      z-index: 9999;
      transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(0.2);
      animation: ripple-effect 0.5s ease-out forwards;
    }
    @keyframes ripple-effect {
      0% { transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(0.2); opacity: 1; }
      100% { transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(2.2); opacity: 0; }
    }
  </style>
